Daybook Entries · A DOCTRINE FOR YOU ONLY, 1584

18 july.

18 july. - Daybook Entries. Records household family, alchemy experiment: Remember on Wednesday night the 18 of July, as I walked alone about 9 of the clock in the evening, in the chamber before my study (above) in divers places of that chamber appeared flashes of fire, and it did not lighten abroad.

Daybook Entries · A DOCTRINE FOR YOU ONLY, 1584

1-9 Aug.

1-9 Aug. - Daybook Entries. Records travel: On Wednesday the first day of August, at afternoon (hora 3) we entered on our journey toward Prague, in the Kingdom of Beame, whither we came on Thursday seven-night after, by 3 of the clock, that is exactly in eight days.

28 Sept. - Daybook Entries. Daybook Entries · TO SALUTE A STRANGE KING, 1584

15 Apr.

15 Apr. - Daybook Entries. Records travel, court patronage, money legal, letters books: I made the Rector privy of the injury I endured, and he courteously sent two Masters of Art with me to the Proconsul to have citations for the landlords to appear on Tuesday by 7 of the clock, to answer to our complaint.

16 Apr.

16 Apr. - Daybook Entries. Records travel, court patronage, household family, money legal: The Lord Laski came to the house, and in the morning would have presently cast all their stuff out of doors, but by entreaty he permitted them to empty all into the lower Stove.

17 Apr.

17 Apr. - Daybook Entries. Records travel, court patronage, household family, money legal: I went with the Lord Laski to the King, to whom I said to this intent: 'Let the comfort, peace and mercy of God be upon you, your most serene Majesty.

19 Apr.

19 Apr. - Daybook Entries. Records a brief diary notice: I took ghostly counsel of Dr Hannibal, the great divine, that had now set out some of his commentaries upon Pymander, Hermetis Trismegisti.

Daybook Entries · TO SALUTE A STRANGE KING, 1584

21 May.

21 May. - Daybook Entries. Records angelic spiritual: I did communicate, and this was the third time within Easter receiving: having had confession first with Dr Hannibal, and twice with Raphael, &c., that all manner of ways I might have a clean and a quiet conscience.

8 Oct.

8 Oct. - Daybook Entries. Records travel, court patronage, household family, money legal, letters books: At my coming home, I bethought me that I would gladly have the said passport, safe conduct or letters of passage more ample and beneficial, as for my wife, children, family, servants and goods whatsoever, and for one whole year to dure from the date of these...
